109	<!-- docs/architecture.md:114 -->
110	### AD-05: An MLP for the compact view, a small CNN for the full grid
111	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `agents`
112	- **Context:** AGENT-03 takes an 11-value compact view. AGENT-05 takes the full grid. QR-02 and QR-03 give each 60 CPU minutes.
113	- **Decision:** Compact view: two hidden layers of 128 units, the same shape the reference uses. Full grid: two 3×3 convolution layers (16 and 32 channels) and one 128-unit hidden layer, over a 3×10×10 head/body/food grid.
114	- **Alternatives:** An MLP on the flattened grid — cheaper per step but slower to learn spatial patterns. It stays the fallback if the CNN misses QR-03.
115	- **Consequences:** The network is chosen in settings (`net = "mlp"` or `"cnn"`), so the fallback is a settings change, not a code change.
116	
117	## Layout (`dt layout rllab`)
118	src/rllab/
119	  __init__.py — rllab: a reinforcement-learning lab for Snake and the fish tank.
120	  agents/
121	    __init__.py — The learning agents.
122	    tabular.py — The table-based value learner (AGENT-01, AGENT-02, ABL-03).
123	    dqn/
124	      __init__.py — The neural value learner.
125	      agent.py — The neural value learner (AGENT-03 to AGENT-05, ABL-01, ABL-02).
126	      networks.py — The value networks: one output per action from a flattened observation (AD-05).
127	  baselines/
128	    __init__.py — The baseline players.
129	    random.py — The random baseline (BASE-01).
130	    scripted.py — The scripted baseline on the compact view (BASE-02).
131	  compare/
132	    __init__.py — Claims between players, tested and reported.
133	    claims.py — Tested claims over pooled scores, and the report (PL-04 step 4).
134	    types.py — A claim to test between two players, and its tested result.
135	  core/
136	    __init__.py — The core: environment and agent interfaces, and run settings.
137	    protocols.py — The two interfaces every environment and every player implements (AD-03, AD-09, AD-13).
138	    settings.py — Run settings from TOML (PL-01 step 1).
139	    types.py — The core's data types: teams, actions, steps, transitions and run settings (AD-09, AD-13).
140	  envs/
141	    __init__.py — The environments: Snake and the corridor.
142	    corridor.py — A 1-D corridor with the goal at its far end (CORE-03).
143	    snake.py — Snake on a square grid, one agent, compact or grid view (SNAKE-01 to SNAKE-09).
144	    types.py — Observation layouts shared with the players that read them.
145	  evaluate/
146	    __init__.py — Held-out greedy evaluation.
147	    protocol.py — Greedy evaluation on held-out seeds (EVAL-01, DATA-01, AGENT-07).
148	    types.py — What one evaluation asks for and what it returns.
149	  lab/
150	    __init__.py — The lab: the command line and the pipelines it runs.
151	    __main__.py — `python -m rllab.lab`: dispatch `train`, `eval`, `replay` and `report`.
152	    build.py — The environment and the agents a run's settings name (PL-01 steps 2 and 7).
153	    cli.py — The command line: `python -m rllab.lab <train | eval | replay | report> ...`.
154	    players.py — Players from run folders or baseline names, scored or opened (PL-03 step 1, PL-04 steps 2-3).
155	    printing.py — One line of scores (PL-03 step 11).
156	    replay.py — The game a replay shows (PL-05 step 2).
157	    report_spec.py — A report's TOML: players, claims and chart groups (PL-04 step 1).
158	    types.py — What a report names: its players, baselines and chart groups (PL-04).
159	    pipelines/
160	      __init__.py — One entry function per pipeline.
161	      pl01_train.py — PL-01: train a run.
162	      pl02_train_reference.py — PL-02: train the reference.
163	      pl03_evaluate.py — PL-03: evaluate.
164	      pl04_report.py — PL-04: report.
165	      pl05_replay.py — PL-05: replay.
166	  reference/
167	    __init__.py — The reference: a library agent trained and played on the lab's environments.
168	    player.py — A trained library model as an Agent (REF-01).
169	    train/
170	      __init__.py — The library agent's training through the gymnasium adapter (AD-03, AD-04).
171	      adapter.py — The one-learner gymnasium adapter over one team of a `core.Env` (AD-03).
172	      mapping.py — The run settings as the library DQN's parameters, mapped one-to-one (AD-04).
173	  runs/
174	    __init__.py — Run folders: settings, curve, checkpoints and recorded games.
175	    store.py — One folder per run: create it, write it as the run goes, read it back (AD-06).
176	    types.py — What a run folder holds: its curve, its recorded games and the run itself (AD-06).
177	  train/
178	    __init__.py — The training loop.
179	    loop.py — The training loop over every team (PL-01 step 15).
180	  viz/
181	    __init__.py — Charts and the replay window.
182	    curves.py — Learning curves with baseline lines (PL-04 step 5).
183	    replay.py — The interactive replay window (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-02).
184	    types.py — A written chart and what it draws.

186	## Interfaces (frozen: build against these, never change them)
187	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:449 -->
188	### compare.ClaimSpec: type
189	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ClaimSpec: name: str; a: str; b: str; direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]; stretch: bool = False; min_ratio: float | None = None`
190	- **Invariants:** `a` and `b` are player names present in the score input. `min_ratio` (REF-02's 0.9) checks `mean(a) ≥ min_ratio · mean(b)` on top of the test.
191	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.ClaimSpec/ · **Cases:** "dqn-compact greater than random"; "dqn-compact not worse than reference with min_ratio 0.9"; the stretch claim for dqn-grid
192	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
193	Sample: tests/contracts/compare.ClaimSpec/ (exists)
194	
195	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:334 -->
196	### evaluate.ScoreSet: type
197	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ScoreSet: player: str; train_seed: int; scores: np.ndarray; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
198	- **Invariants:** `scores` has one float score per evaluation game (food eaten, or mean survival steps per fish). `games` holds the first `record` games played.
199	- **Boundary:** compare, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.ScoreSet/ · **Cases:** 200 scripted-player scores for seed 0; 200 random-player scores for seed 0; 200 scripted-fish scores for seed 0
200	- **Used by:** compare, train, lab; PL-03, PL-04
201	Sample: tests/contracts/evaluate.ScoreSet/ (exists)
202	
203	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:301 -->
204	### runs.RunRecord: type
205	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: path: Path; settings: RunSettings; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]; checkpoints: dict[str, Path]; generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
206	- **Invariants:** `settings` round-trips through `settings.json` unchanged. `checkpoints` holds one final checkpoint per learning team (single-team runs: `SOLO`), present when `status` is `complete`. `generations` holds each learning team's numbered generations in order (10 when `settings.generations == 10`, else empty) (RUN-02, SELF-04).
207	- **Boundary:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete tabular corridor run; an incomplete Snake run with 3 curve points; a complete predator-prey rung-3 run with 10 generations per team
208	- **Used by:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
209	Sample: tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ (exists)
210	
211	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:455 -->
212	### compare.Claim: type
213	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Claim: spec: ClaimSpec; mean_a: float; mean_b: float; p_value: float; p_corrected: float; passed: bool; method: str`
214	- **Invariants:** `method` names what the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill chose (statistic, null distribution, correction). For `greater`, `passed` means significant at 0.05 after correction. For `not_worse`, it means "a worse than b" is not significant, and `min_ratio` holds when set.
215	- **Boundary:** lab, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Claim/ · **Cases:** a passed "greater" claim; a failed "not_worse" claim; a stretch claim that failed
216	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
217	Sample: tests/contracts/compare.Claim/ (exists)
218	
219	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:470 -->
220	### viz.Chart: type
221	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: path: Path; groups: tuple[str, ...]; baselines: tuple[str, ...]`
222	- **Invariants:** `path` is a written PNG. `groups` and `baselines` list what the chart draws, in legend order.
223	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.Chart/ · **Cases:** the ladder chart (three agents and the reference, two baselines); the ablation chart
224	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
225	Sample: tests/contracts/viz.Chart/ (exists)
226	
227	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:461 -->
228	### compare.run_claims: Tested claims and report
229	`def run_claims(scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec], *, alpha: float = 0.05, charts: Sequence[Chart] = (), out: Path) -> list[Claim]`
230	- **Invariants:** each player's scores are pooled across its 5 training seeds. The correction covers every spec in one call. Writes `out/report.md` listing every player's mean and spread (BASE-03) and every claim, with stretch claims labelled "stretch" (EVAL-05), and links each chart in `charts` by its path under a "Cross-play" section (EVAL-11).
231	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a spec names a player missing from `scores`
232	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.run_claims/ · **Cases:** a fake returning one passed and one failed Claim
233	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
234	Sample: tests/contracts/compare.run_claims/ (exists)
235	
236	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:476 -->
237	### viz.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
238	`def plot_curves(groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]], *, scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], baselines: Sequence[str], out: Path) -> Chart`
239	- **Invariants:** one line per group (the mean over its seeds, with a min–max band). Each name in `baselines` gets a horizontal line at its pooled mean from `scores` (EVAL-06). Several groups on one chart form the ablation comparison (ABL-04).
240	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a baseline name is missing from `scores`
241	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the ladder Chart
242	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
243	Sample: tests/contracts/viz.plot_curves/ (exists)

57	## Review
58	Run by the reviewer agent: the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
59	
60	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
61	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
62	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
63	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistic, never holding a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows. Each fake passes its contract's cases; anything it does beyond echoing a fixture (filtering, ordering, an error) is a case the real provider must pass too. Contract tests check shape only, never a stub or other state a later task changes.
64	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
65	
66	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
67	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
68	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
69	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
70	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
71	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
72	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
73	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double; fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
74	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
75	
76	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
77	
78	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a contract test pinning a transient state, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.

"""Run settings from TOML (PL-01 step 1)."""

import tomllib
from dataclasses import MISSING, fields, replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, get_args, get_origin

from rllab.core.types import AgentParams, Exploration, Rewards, RunSettings

_SECTIONS: dict[str, type[Rewards] | type[Exploration] | type[AgentParams]] = {
    "rewards": Rewards,
    "exploration": Exploration,
    "params": AgentParams,
}
_TOP_DEFAULTS: dict[str, Any] = {
    "n_agents": 1,
    "self_play": "none",
    "pool_latest": 0.5,
    "ablation": "none",
    "opponents": {},
    "rewards": {},
    "exploration": {},
}
_TANK_REWARDS: dict[str, float] = {"step": 1.0, "death": 0.0}
_VIEWS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "snake": ("compact", "grid"),
    "corridor": ("index",),
    "tank": ("full", "limited"),
    "predprey": ("limited",),
}
_AGENT_ENVS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "tabular": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "dqn": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "reinforce": ("tank",),
    "ppo": ("tank", "predprey"),
    "random": ("snake", "corridor", "tank"),
    "scripted": ("snake", "corridor", "tank"),
    "reference": ("snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"),
}
_ABLATION_AGENT: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "none": tuple(_AGENT_ENVS),
    "no_memory": ("dqn",),
    "no_target": ("dqn",),
    "no_explore": ("tabular",),
}
_TEAMS = frozenset({"predator", "prey"})
_OPPONENT_KINDS = ("learn", "scripted", "random")


def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings:
    """Read a TOML settings file into fully resolved `RunSettings`."""
    table = _with_defaults(tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    _check_names(RunSettings, table, "")
    for section, cls in _SECTIONS.items():
        _check_names(cls, table[section], f"{section}.")
    _check_opponents(table["opponents"])
    settings = RunSettings(
        **{
            **table,
            "opponents": dict(table["opponents"]),
            "rewards": Rewards(**table["rewards"]),
            "exploration": Exploration(**table["exploration"]),
            "params": AgentParams(**table["params"]),
        }
    )
    _check_values(settings)
    return settings


def with_seed(settings: RunSettings, seed: int) -> RunSettings:
    """The same settings with only `seed` replaced."""
    return replace(settings, seed=seed)


def _check_names(cls: type, table: dict[str, Any], prefix: str) -> None:
    """Raise `ValueError` naming the first unknown, missing or unknown-valued key of `table`."""
    known = {f.name: f for f in fields(cls)}
    for key in table:
        if key not in known:
            raise ValueError(f"{prefix}{key}: unknown setting")
    for name, field in known.items():
        if name not in table:
            if field.default is MISSING:
                raise ValueError(f"{prefix}{name}: missing setting")
            continue
        if get_origin(field.type) is Literal and table[name] not in get_args(field.type):
            raise ValueError(f"{prefix}{name}: unknown value {table[name]!r}")


def _check_opponents(opponents: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for team, kind in opponents.items():
        if kind not in _OPPONENT_KINDS:
            raise ValueError(f"opponents.{team}: unknown value {kind!r}")


def _with_defaults(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """`raw` with every unspecified top-level setting at its default, and the tank's rewards."""
    table = {**_TOP_DEFAULTS, **raw}
    learns = "learn" in table["opponents"].values()
    table.setdefault("generations", 10 if learns else 0)
    if table.get("env") == "tank":
        table["rewards"] = {**_TANK_REWARDS, **table["rewards"]}
    return table


def _check_values(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    """Raise `ValueError` naming every key whose value breaks the `RunSettings` invariants."""
    learners = sum(kind == "learn" for kind in s.opponents.values())
    rules = (
        ("seed", 0 <= s.seed < 1_000_000),
        ("steps", s.steps > 0),
        ("eval_every", s.eval_every > 0),
        ("eval_games", s.eval_games > 0),
        ("view", s.view in _VIEWS[s.env]),
        ("n_agents", 1 <= s.n_agents <= (16 if s.env == "tank" else 1)),
        ("agent", s.env in _AGENT_ENVS[s.agent]),
        ("agent", s.agent != "reference" or s.env != "tank" or s.n_agents == 1),
        ("view", s.agent != "reference" or s.env != "tank" or s.view == "full"),
        ("ablation", s.agent in _ABLATION_AGENT[s.ablation]),
        ("opponents", set(s.opponents) == (_TEAMS if s.env == "predprey" else set())),
        ("self_play", (s.self_play != "none") == (learners == len(_TEAMS))),
        ("generations", s.generations == (10 if learners else 0)),
        ("pool_latest", 0 <= s.pool_latest <= 1),
        ("params.gamma", 0 < s.params.gamma <= 1),
        ("exploration", 0 <= s.exploration.end <= s.exploration.start <= 1),
    )
    bad = sorted({key for key, ok in rules if not ok})
    if bad:
        raise ValueError(f"settings out of range: {', '.join(bad)}")
"""The command line: `python -m rllab.lab <train | eval | replay | report> ...`."""

import argparse
import os
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import load_settings
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl02_train_reference import run_train_reference
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl03_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl04_report import run_report
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl05_replay import run_replay


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one subcommand; 0 on success, 2 on bad settings or a missing run."""
    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.smoke:
        os.environ["MPLBACKEND"] = "Agg"  # headless: charts are written, no window opens
    try:
        args.command(args)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    return 0


def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """PL-01 per seed, or PL-02 when the settings name the reference agent."""
    path = Path(args.settings)
    settings = load_settings(path)
    entry = run_train_reference if settings.agent == "reference" else run_train
    for seed in seed_range(args.seeds, default=settings.seed):
        entry(path, seed)


def _eval(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    for target in args.targets:
        run_evaluate(target, args.seed)


def _replay(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    if args.new and args.seed is None:
        raise ValueError("--new needs --seed S")
    run_replay(Path(args.run), game=args.game, new_seed=args.seed if args.new else None)


def _report(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    spec = Path(args.spec)
    run_report(spec, out=spec.parent)


def seed_range(text: str | None, *, default: int) -> list[int]:
    """`"0-4"` is seeds 0 to 4, `"3"` is seed 3, and no text is `default`."""
    if text is None:
        return [default]
    first, _, last = text.partition("-")
    seeds = list(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))
    if not seeds:
        raise ValueError(f"--seeds {text!r} names no seed")
    return seeds


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    common = argparse.ArgumentParser(add_help=False)
    common.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="headless smoke run")
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m rllab.lab")
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(required=True)

    train = commands.add_parser("train", parents=[common], help="train one run per seed")
    train.add_argument("settings", help="settings TOML file")
    train.add_argument("--seeds", help="seeds, such as 0-4 (default: the settings' seed)")
    train.set_defaults(command=_train)

    evaluate = commands.add_parser("eval", parents=[common], help="score players")
    evaluate.add_argument("targets", nargs="+", help="run folders, random or scripted")
    evaluate.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0, help="training seed to score on")
    evaluate.set_defaults(command=_eval)

    replay = commands.add_parser("replay", parents=[common], help="replay a recorded game")
    replay.add_argument("run", help="run folder")
    which = replay.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    which.add_argument("--game", type=int, default=0, help="recorded game index")
    which.add_argument("--new", action="store_true", help="play a new game on --seed")
    replay.add_argument("--seed", type=int, help="seed of the new game")
    replay.set_defaults(command=_replay)

    report = commands.add_parser("report", parents=[common], help="test claims, draw charts")
    report.add_argument("spec", help="report TOML file; outputs go beside it")
    report.set_defaults(command=_report)
    return parser
"""`python -m rllab.lab`: dispatch `train`, `eval`, `replay` and `report`."""

from rllab.lab.cli import main

raise SystemExit(main())

"""The core's data types: teams, actions, steps, transitions and run settings (AD-09, AD-13)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

SOLO: str = "agents"
"""The one team name every single-team environment uses."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ActionSpace:
    """Discrete: `n >= 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim >= 1` and `n == 0`."""

    kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]
    n: int = 0
    dim: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """One team's size, per-agent observation shape and shared action space."""

    n_agents: int
    obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]
    actions: ActionSpace


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepResult:
    """One environment step, each map keyed by team and batch-first over that team's agents."""

    obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    done: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    terminated: bool
    truncated: bool
    info: dict[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One learning team's step, batch-first; `terminated` is never set by truncation."""

    obs: np.ndarray
    actions: np.ndarray
    reward: np.ndarray
    next_obs: np.ndarray
    terminated: np.ndarray
    active: np.ndarray
    truncated: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rewards:
    """Reward sizes; for the corridor `food` is the goal reward."""

    food: float = 1.0
    death: float = -1.0
    step: float = 0.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Exploration:
    """A rate falling linearly from `start` to `end` over `decay_steps`, then constant."""

    start: float = 1.0
    end: float = 0.05
    decay_steps: int = 50_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentParams:
    """Learner hyperparameters; each agent reads the fields it uses."""

    lr: float
    gamma: float
    batch_size: int = 64
    memory_size: int = 50_000
    target_every: int = 1_000
    learn_start: int = 1_000
    hidden: int = 128
    net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"
    rollout_steps: int = 2048
    epochs: int = 10
    clip: float = 0.2
    gae_lambda: float = 0.95
    games_per_update: int = 10


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunSettings:
    """Everything one run needs, fully resolved (RUN-01)."""

    name: str
    env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]
    view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]
    n_agents: int
    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]
    self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
    pool_latest: float
    generations: int
    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
    rewards: Rewards
    exploration: Exploration
    params: AgentParams
    steps: int
    seed: int
    eval_every: int
    eval_games: int
"""A report's TOML: players, claims and chart groups (PL-04 step 1).

A report file looks like:

    ladder = ["dqn-compact"]

    [ablations]
    dqn-no-memory = "dqn-compact"

    [players.dqn-compact]
    runs = ["runs/dqn-compact-s0", "runs/dqn-compact-s1"]
    seeds = [0, 1]

    [players.random]
    baseline = "random"
    seeds = [0, 1]

    [[claims]]
    name = "dqn-compact greater than random"
    a = "dqn-compact"
    b = "random"
    direction = "greater"

A player gives either `runs` (one run folder per seed) or `baseline` (a baseline name scored
on every seed). Players given by `baseline` are the report's baselines.
"""

import tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from rllab.compare import ClaimSpec
from rllab.lab.types import PlayerTarget, ReportTargets

_DIRECTIONS = ("greater", "not_worse")


def load_report_spec(path: Path) -> tuple[tuple[ClaimSpec, ...], ReportTargets]:
    """The claims and the targets a report TOML names."""
    raw = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    players = tuple(_player(name, table) for name, table in raw.get("players", {}).items())
    named = {p.name for p in players}
    claims = tuple(_claim(table, named) for table in raw.get("claims", []))
    ladder = tuple(raw.get("ladder", []))
    ablations = dict(raw.get("ablations", {}))
    _check_listed("ladder", ladder, named)
    _check_listed("ablations", (*ablations, *ablations.values()), named)
    baselines = tuple(name for name, table in raw.get("players", {}).items() if "baseline" in table)
    targets = ReportTargets(
        players=players, baselines=baselines, ladder=ladder, ablations=ablations
    )
    return claims, targets


def _player(name: str, table: dict[str, Any]) -> PlayerTarget:
    """One player from its table: `runs` (one per seed) or a `baseline` name, and `seeds`."""
    seeds = tuple(table["seeds"])
    if ("runs" in table) == ("baseline" in table):
        raise ValueError(f"players.{name}: give exactly one of `runs` or `baseline`")
    if "baseline" in table:
        return PlayerTarget(name=name, targets=(table["baseline"],) * len(seeds), seeds=seeds)
    runs = tuple(table["runs"])
    if len(runs) != len(seeds):
        raise ValueError(f"players.{name}: {len(runs)} runs for {len(seeds)} seeds")
    return PlayerTarget(name=name, targets=runs, seeds=seeds)


def _claim(table: dict[str, Any], named: set[str]) -> ClaimSpec:
    """One claim; `ValueError` if it names an unlisted player or an unknown direction."""
    _check_listed(f"claims.{table['name']}", (table["a"], table["b"]), named)
    direction = table["direction"]
    if direction not in _DIRECTIONS:
        raise ValueError(f"claims.{table['name']}: unknown direction {direction!r}")
    return ClaimSpec(
        name=table["name"],
        a=table["a"],
        b=table["b"],
        direction="greater" if direction == "greater" else "not_worse",
        stretch=table.get("stretch", False),
        min_ratio=table.get("min_ratio"),
    )


def _check_listed(where: str, names: tuple[str, ...], named: set[str]) -> None:
    """Raise `ValueError` naming each of `names` that is not a listed player."""
    missing = sorted(set(names) - named)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{where}: unlisted players {', '.join(missing)}")
"""What a report names: its players, baselines and chart groups (PL-04)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from rllab.runs import RunRecord


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlayerTarget:
    """One report player: `targets[i]` (a run folder or a baseline name) scored on `seeds[i]`."""

    name: str
    targets: tuple[str, ...]
    seeds: tuple[int, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReportTargets:
    """The players to score, the baselines drawn as lines, and each chart's player names.

    `ablations` maps each ablation player to its full agent's player.
    """

    players: tuple[PlayerTarget, ...]
    baselines: tuple[str, ...]
    ladder: tuple[str, ...]
    ablations: dict[str, str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurveRuns:
    """Each chart's groups: player name to its runs, one per training seed."""

    ladder: dict[str, tuple[RunRecord, ...]]
    ablations: dict[str, tuple[RunRecord, ...]]
"""Players from run folders or baseline names, scored or opened (PL-03 step 1, PL-04 steps 2-3)."""

from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Rewards, RunSettings
from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, ScoreSet, evaluate
from rllab.lab.build import make_agent, make_env
from rllab.lab.types import CurveRuns, PlayerTarget, ReportTargets
from rllab.reference import ReferencePlayer
from rllab.runs import RunRecord, open_run

_BASELINES: tuple[Literal["random", "scripted"], ...] = ("random", "scripted")

# A baseline plays default Snake on the compact view. It never learns, so `params`, `steps`
# and the evaluation cadence are the ladder's and go unused.
_BASELINE_SETTINGS: dict[str, RunSettings] = {
    agent: RunSettings(
        name=agent,
        env="snake",
        view="compact",
        agent=agent,
        n_agents=1,
        opponents={},
        self_play="none",
        pool_latest=0.5,
        generations=0,
        ablation="none",
        rewards=Rewards(),
        exploration=Exploration(),
        params=AgentParams(lr=0.001, gamma=0.99),
        steps=300_000,
        seed=0,
        eval_every=10_000,
        eval_games=50,
    )
    for agent in _BASELINES
}


def load_player(target: str, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, Agent], RunSettings]:
    """One agent per team for a run folder's checkpoint or a baseline name, and its settings."""
    if target in _BASELINE_SETTINGS:
        settings = replace(_BASELINE_SETTINGS[target], seed=seed)
        return make_agent(settings, make_env(settings)()), settings
    record = open_run(Path(target))
    if not record.checkpoints:
        raise ValueError(f"run {target}: no checkpoint (status {record.status})")
    if record.settings.agent == "reference":
        references: dict[str, Agent] = {
            team: ReferencePlayer.from_run(record, team=team) for team in record.checkpoints
        }
        return references, record.settings
    agents = make_agent(record.settings, make_env(record.settings)())
    for team, checkpoint in record.checkpoints.items():
        agents[team].load(checkpoint)
    return agents, record.settings


def score_players(targets: ReportTargets) -> dict[str, tuple[ScoreSet, ...]]:
    """One ScoreSet per player and seed."""
    return {
        player.name: tuple(
            _score(player.name, target, seed)
            for target, seed in zip(player.targets, player.seeds, strict=True)
        )
        for player in targets.players
    }


def open_curve_runs(targets: ReportTargets) -> CurveRuns:
    """The ladder's and the ablations' runs; `ValueError` if an ablation's seeds or steps differ."""
    players = {player.name: player for player in targets.players}
    ablation_names = [name for pair in targets.ablations.items() for name in pair]
    ablations = {name: _open_runs(players[name]) for name in dict.fromkeys(ablation_names)}
    for ablation, full in targets.ablations.items():
        _check_matches(ablation, ablations[ablation], full, ablations[full])
    return CurveRuns(
        ladder={name: _open_runs(players[name]) for name in targets.ladder},
        ablations=ablations,
    )


def _check_matches(
    ablation: str, runs: tuple[RunRecord, ...], full: str, full_runs: tuple[RunRecord, ...]
) -> None:
    """`ValueError` naming `seeds` or `steps` if the ablation's runs differ from its full agent's."""
    seeds = sorted(run.settings.seed for run in runs)
    full_seeds = sorted(run.settings.seed for run in full_runs)
    if seeds != full_seeds:
        raise ValueError(f"ablation {ablation}: seeds {seeds} differ from {full}'s {full_seeds}")
    steps = {run.settings.steps for run in runs}
    full_steps = {run.settings.steps for run in full_runs}
    if steps != full_steps:
        raise ValueError(
            f"ablation {ablation}: steps {sorted(steps)} differ from {full}'s {sorted(full_steps)}"
        )


def _score(name: str, target: str, seed: int) -> ScoreSet:
    """PL-03 steps 1, 9 and 10 for one target, scored under the player's name."""
    agents, settings = load_player(target, seed)
    return evaluate(make_env(settings), agents, EvalSpec(player=name, train_seed=seed))


def _open_runs(player: PlayerTarget) -> tuple[RunRecord, ...]:
    return tuple(open_run(Path(target)) for target in player.targets)
"""One entry function per pipeline."""
"""PL-01: train a run."""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core.settings import load_settings, with_seed
from rllab.lab.build import make_agent, make_env
from rllab.runs import RunRecord
from rllab.train import train


def run_train(settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord:
    """Train the agent the settings file names, on one seed."""
    settings = load_settings(settings_path)
    settings = with_seed(settings, seed)
    make = make_env(settings)
    agents = make_agent(settings, make())
    return train(make, agents, settings)
"""PL-02: train the reference."""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import load_settings
from rllab.core.settings import with_seed
from rllab.reference import train_reference
from rllab.runs import RunRecord


def run_train_reference(settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord:
    """Train the library agent the settings file names, on one seed."""
    settings = load_settings(settings_path)
    settings = with_seed(settings, seed)
    return train_reference(settings)
"""PL-03: evaluate."""

from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, ScoreSet, evaluate
from rllab.lab.build import make_env
from rllab.lab.players import load_player
from rllab.lab.printing import print_scores


def run_evaluate(target: str, seed: int) -> ScoreSet:
    """Score a run folder or a baseline name on the held-out games of `seed`."""
    agents, settings = load_player(target, seed)
    make = make_env(settings)
    scores = evaluate(make, agents, EvalSpec(player=target, train_seed=seed))
    return print_scores(scores)
"""PL-04: report."""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.compare import Claim, run_claims
from rllab.lab.players import open_curve_runs, score_players
from rllab.lab.report_spec import load_report_spec
from rllab.viz import plot_curves


def run_report(spec_path: Path, out: Path) -> list[Claim]:
    """Score the report's players, test its claims and draw its charts into `out`."""
    specs, targets = load_report_spec(spec_path)
    scores = score_players(targets)
    curves = open_curve_runs(targets)
    claims = run_claims(scores, specs, out=out)
    plot_curves(curves.ladder, scores=scores, baselines=targets.baselines, out=out / "ladder.png")
    plot_curves(
        curves.ablations, scores=scores, baselines=targets.baselines, out=out / "ablations.png"
    )
    return claims
"""PL-05: replay."""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.lab.replay import pick_game
from rllab.runs import open_run
from rllab.viz import Replay


def run_replay(run: Path, game: int = 0, new_seed: int | None = None) -> None:
    """Show a run's recorded game, or a new one on `new_seed`."""
    record = open_run(run)
    recording = pick_game(record, game, new_seed)
    Replay(recording).show()
"""One folder per run: create it, write it as the run goes, read it back (AD-06)."""

import csv
import json
import zipfile
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Rewards, RunSettings
from rllab.runs.types import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord

_CURVE_FIELDS = ("step", "games", "mean_score", "elapsed_s")
_STATUSES: dict[str, Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]] = {
    "running": "running",
    "complete": "complete",
    "incomplete": "incomplete",
}
_BOARDS: dict[str, Literal["grid", "tank"]] = {"grid": "grid", "tank": "tank"}
_CHECKPOINT_SUFFIX = {"tabular": ".npz", "reference": ".zip"}


class RunWriter:
    """Writes one run folder; each curve point is flushed at once."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path, settings: RunSettings) -> None:
        self.path = path
        self.settings = settings

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        with (self.path / "curve.csv").open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            csv.writer(f).writerow([p.step, p.games, repr(p.mean_score), repr(p.elapsed_s)])

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
        stem = "checkpoint" if team == SOLO else f"checkpoint-{team}"
        agent.save(self.path / (stem + _CHECKPOINT_SUFFIX.get(self.settings.agent, ".pt")))

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        path = self.path / "generations" / team / str(index)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        agent.save(path)
        return path

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        arrays: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for i, game in enumerate(games):
            arrays[f"{i}.seed"] = np.asarray(game.seed, dtype=np.int64)
            arrays[f"{i}.board"] = np.asarray(game.board)
            arrays[f"{i}.frames"] = game.frames
            arrays[f"{i}.rewards"] = game.rewards
            for team, scores in game.scores.items():
                arrays[f"{i}.scores.{team}"] = scores
        np.savez(self.path / "games.npz", **arrays)

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        (self.path / "status").write_text(status, encoding="utf-8")
        return open_run(self.path)


def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter:
    """Start a uniquely named run folder holding `settings.json` and `status=running`."""
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S")
    path = _new_folder(root, f"{settings.name}-s{settings.seed}-{stamp}")
    (path / "settings.json").write_text(json.dumps(asdict(settings), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    (path / "curve.csv").write_text(",".join(_CURVE_FIELDS) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (path / "status").write_text("running", encoding="utf-8")
    return RunWriter(path, settings)


def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    """Read a run folder back (PL-05 step 1)."""
    settings_file = path / "settings.json"
    if not settings_file.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"run {path}: no settings.json")
    return RunRecord(
        path=path,
        settings=_settings_from_json(json.loads(settings_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))),
        curve=_read_curve(path / "curve.csv"),
        status=_read_status(path / "status"),
        checkpoints=_find_checkpoints(path),
        generations=_find_generations(path / "generations"),
        games=_read_games(path / "games.npz"),
    )


def _new_folder(root: Path, stem: str) -> Path:
    """`root/stem`, or `root/stem-<n>` with the first free `n` when it exists."""
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    n = 0
    while True:
        path = root / (stem if n == 0 else f"{stem}-{n}")
        try:
            path.mkdir()
        except FileExistsError:
            n += 1
            continue
        return path


def _settings_from_json(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> RunSettings:
    return RunSettings(
        **{
            **raw,
            "rewards": Rewards(**raw["rewards"]),
            "exploration": Exploration(**raw["exploration"]),
            "params": AgentParams(**raw["params"]),
        }
    )


def _read_curve(path: Path) -> tuple[CurvePoint, ...]:
    if not path.is_file():
        return ()
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return tuple(
            CurvePoint(
                step=int(row["step"]),
                games=int(row["games"]),
                mean_score=float(row["mean_score"]),
                elapsed_s=float(row["elapsed_s"]),
            )
            for row in csv.DictReader(f)
        )


def _read_status(path: Path) -> Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if text not in _STATUSES:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: unknown status {text!r}")
    return _STATUSES[text]


def _find_checkpoints(run: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Each team's final checkpoint: `checkpoint.<ext>` is `SOLO`'s, `checkpoint-<team>.<ext>`."""
    found = {}
    for path in sorted(run.glob("checkpoint*")):
        team = path.stem.removeprefix("checkpoint").removeprefix("-")
        found[team or SOLO] = path
    return found


def _find_generations(folder: Path) -> dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]:
    if not folder.is_dir():
        return {}
    return {
        team.name: tuple(sorted(team.iterdir(), key=lambda p: int(p.name)))
        for team in sorted(folder.iterdir())
    }


def _read_games(path: Path) -> tuple[GameRecording, ...]:
    if not path.is_file():
        return ()
    try:
        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as data:
            arrays = {key: data[key] for key in data.files}
        count = sum(key.endswith(".seed") for key in arrays)
        return tuple(_game_from_arrays(arrays, i) for i in range(count))
    except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, zipfile.BadZipFile) as error:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: unreadable recording ({error})") from error


def _game_from_arrays(arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray], i: int) -> GameRecording:
    prefix = f"{i}.scores."
    return GameRecording(
        seed=int(arrays[f"{i}.seed"]),
        board=_BOARDS[str(arrays[f"{i}.board"])],
        frames=arrays[f"{i}.frames"],
        rewards=arrays[f"{i}.rewards"],
        scores={
            key.removeprefix(prefix): value
            for key, value in arrays.items()
            if key.startswith(prefix)
        },
    )

164	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then

167	  fi

169	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
170	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then

172	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
173	  fi

221	  done

226	  run ruff format --check .

238	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0

240	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
241	    run typos "${files[@]}"
242	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
243	    ;;
244	  collect)

247	      exit 1

282	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086

284	    ;;

287	    ;;
288	  *)

290	    exit 64
291	    ;;
292	esac

"""Intent tests for envs.Corridor (M1-T14): TC-11 corridor rules (CORE-03)."""

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, Rewards, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs import Corridor

FIXTURE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "fixtures" / "rllab" / "TC-11"
SEED = 0


def _load(name: str) -> Any:
    return json.loads((FIXTURE / name).read_text())


@pytest.fixture
def tc11_input() -> Any:
    return _load("input.json")


@pytest.fixture
def tc11_expected() -> Any:
    return _load("expected.json")


def _corridor(spec: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Corridor:
    return Corridor(length=spec["length"], max_steps=spec["max_steps"], **kwargs)


def _act(action: int) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    return {SOLO: np.array([action], dtype=np.int64)}


def _position(obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]) -> int:
    row = obs[SOLO][0]
    assert row.sum() == 1.0
    return int(np.argmax(row))


@pytest.mark.intent
def test_tc11a_eight_rights_reach_cell_nine_and_ninth_reaches_goal(tc11_input, tc11_expected):
    expected = tc11_expected["a"]
    actions = tc11_input["sequences"]["a"]
    env = _corridor(tc11_input)
    env.reset(SEED)
    result = None
    for action in actions[:-1]:
        result = env.step(_act(action))
        assert not result.terminated
        assert not result.truncated
    assert result is not None
    assert _position(result.obs) == expected["position_after_8"]

    final = env.step(_act(actions[-1]))
    assert final.reward[SOLO][0] == expected["final_reward"]
    assert final.terminated is expected["terminated"]
    assert final.truncated is False
    assert final.info["steps"] == expected["steps"]
    assert bool(final.done[SOLO][0]) is True


@pytest.mark.intent
def test_tc11a_no_reward_before_the_goal(tc11_input):
    env = _corridor(tc11_input)
    env.reset(SEED)
    for action in tc11_input["sequences"]["a"][:-1]:
        result = env.step(_act(action))
        assert result.reward[SOLO][0] == 0.0
        assert bool(result.done[SOLO][0]) is False


@pytest.mark.intent
def test_tc11b_left_at_the_start_leaves_the_observation_unchanged(tc11_input, tc11_expected):
    env = _corridor(tc11_input)
    obs0, _ = env.reset(SEED)

# rllab — Tests

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Pipelines:** [pipelines.md](pipelines.md)

Board coordinates are `(row, col)` with row 0 at the top, so "up" decreases the row. Bodies are listed head first. Boards are set with `Snake.reset_to`. Snake and corridor cases address agent 0 of a batch of one (AD-09): an action list is one action per step, and an expected observation is agent 0's row.

Tank positions are `(x, y)` in tank radii with the centre at the origin, set with `FishTank.reset_to`. The ball reflects when its edge reaches the wall, that is, when its centre is 0.92 from the origin. Predator-prey positions use the same frame, set with `PredatorPrey.reset_to`; each case's `input.json` lists all 6 prey; unless a case says otherwise, the other prey sit at `(−0.5, 0)`, `(0, 0.5)`, `(0, −0.5)`, `(−0.4, 0.4)`, `(−0.4, −0.4)` and the 8 pellets on the circle of radius 0.9 at 45° steps from `(0.9, 0)`, every animal at rest.

## Strategy
| Pipeline | Smoke | Step unit tests | Golden cases | Model eval |
|---|---|---|---|---|

"""Greedy evaluation on held-out seeds (EVAL-01, DATA-01, AGENT-07)."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Env
from rllab.evaluate.types import EvalSpec, ScoreSet
from rllab.runs import GameRecording

HELD_OUT_FLOOR = 1_000_000  # every training seed is below this (DATA-01)
SEEDS_PER_TRAIN_SEED = 1000


def eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]:
    """`1_000_000 + 1000 * train_seed + i` for `i < n`: never a training seed."""
    if not 0 <= n <= SEEDS_PER_TRAIN_SEED:
        raise ValueError(f"n must be within 0..{SEEDS_PER_TRAIN_SEED}, got {n}")
    if not 0 <= train_seed < HELD_OUT_FLOOR:
        raise ValueError(f"train seed must be within 0..{HELD_OUT_FLOOR - 1}, got {train_seed}")
    first = HELD_OUT_FLOOR + SEEDS_PER_TRAIN_SEED * train_seed
    return list(range(first, first + n))


def evaluate(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], spec: EvalSpec) -> ScoreSet:
    """Play `spec.n_games` greedy games with one agent per team, scoring `spec.team`."""
    env = make_env()
    scores: list[float] = []
    games: list[GameRecording] = []
    for i, seed in enumerate(eval_seeds(spec.train_seed, spec.n_games)):
        score, game = _play(env, agents, seed, team=spec.team, record=i < spec.record)
        scores.append(score)
        if game is not None:
            games.append(game)
    return ScoreSet(
        player=spec.player,
        train_seed=spec.train_seed,
        scores=np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64),
        games=tuple(games),
    )


def _play(
    env: Env, agents: Mapping[str, Agent], seed: int, *, team: str, record: bool
) -> tuple[float, GameRecording | None]:
    """One greedy game on `seed`: `team`'s final score, and the game itself when `record`."""
    obs, info = env.reset(seed)
    frames = [env.frame()] if record else []
    rewards: list[float] = []
    history = {name: [info[f"score:{name}"]] for name in env.teams}
    over = False
    while not over:
        actions = {name: agents[name].act(obs[name], explore=False) for name in env.teams}
        result = env.step(actions)
        obs, info = result.obs, result.info
        over = result.terminated or result.truncated
        rewards.append(sum(float(r.sum()) for r in result.reward.values()))
        for name, scores in history.items():
            scores.append(info[f"score:{name}"])
        if record:
            frames.append(env.frame())
    score = float(info[f"score:{team}"])
    if not record:
        return score, None
    return score, GameRecording(
        seed=seed,
        board=env.board,
        frames=np.stack(frames),
        rewards=np.array(rewards, dtype=np.float32),
        scores={name: np.array(s, dtype=np.float32) for name, s in history.items()},
    )
"""What one evaluation asks for and what it returns."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import GameRecording


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalSpec:
    """`n_games` games of `team` on `eval_seeds(train_seed, n_games)`, the first `record` recorded."""

    player: str
    train_seed: int
    team: str = SOLO
    n_games: int = 200
    record: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.n_games < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"n_games must be at least 1, got {self.n_games}")
        if not 0 <= self.record <= self.n_games:
            raise ValueError(f"record must be within 0..{self.n_games}, got {self.record}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScoreSet:
    """One score per evaluation game, and the first recorded games."""

    player: str
    train_seed: int
    scores: np.ndarray
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]
"""The training loop over every team (PL-01 step 15)."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from time import monotonic

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, Env, RunSettings, StepResult, Transition
from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, evaluate
from rllab.runs import CurvePoint, RunRecord, RunWriter, create_run

RECORDED_GAMES = 5
PROGRESS_EVERY_S = 30.0
TRAINING_SEEDS = 1_000_000  # game seeds stay below every evaluation seed (DATA-01)


def train(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    settings: RunSettings,
    *,
    root: Path = Path("runs"),
) -> RunRecord:
    """Step for `settings.steps` steps, learning after each, with a curve point every `eval_every`."""
    writer = create_run(settings, root=root)
    try:
        _run_steps(make_env, agents, settings, writer)
        _save_results(make_env, agents, settings, writer)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        writer.finish("incomplete")
        raise
    return writer.finish("complete")


def learning_teams(settings: RunSettings) -> list[str]:
    """The teams that learn: `SOLO` in a single-team run, else those `opponents` marks `learn`."""
    if not settings.opponents:
        return [SOLO]
    return [team for team, role in settings.opponents.items() if role == "learn"]


def _run_steps(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    settings: RunSettings,
    writer: RunWriter,
) -> None:
    """The step loop: act for every team, then each learning team learns from its Transition."""
    learners = learning_teams(settings)
    spec = EvalSpec(settings.name, settings.seed, team=learners[0], n_games=settings.eval_games)
    env = make_env()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(settings.seed)
    start = last_print = monotonic()
    games = 0
    obs, _ = env.reset(int(rng.integers(TRAINING_SEEDS)))
    active = _all_active(obs)
    for step in range(settings.steps):
        if step % settings.eval_every == 0:
            score = _mean_score(make_env, agents, spec)
            writer.add_point(CurvePoint(step, games, score, monotonic() - start))
        result = _step_and_learn(env, agents, learners, obs, active=active)
        if result.terminated or result.truncated:
            games += 1
            obs, _ = env.reset(int(rng.integers(TRAINING_SEEDS)))
            active = _all_active(obs)
        else:
            obs = result.obs
            active = {team: ~done for team, done in result.done.items()}
        if monotonic() - last_print >= PROGRESS_EVERY_S:
            last_print = monotonic()
            print(_progress(settings, step + 1, games, last_print - start), flush=True)
    score = _mean_score(make_env, agents, spec)
    writer.add_point(CurvePoint(settings.steps, games, score, monotonic() - start))


def _step_and_learn(
    env: Env,
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    learners: list[str],
    obs: Mapping[str, np.ndarray],
    *,
    active: Mapping[str, np.ndarray],
) -> StepResult:
    """Every team acts (exploring), the env steps, then each learner learns from its step."""
    actions = {team: agents[team].act(obs[team], explore=True) for team in env.teams}
    result = env.step(actions)
    for team in learners:
        agents[team].learn(_transition(team, obs, actions, result, active=active[team]))
    return result


def _all_active(obs: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    return {team: np.ones(len(batch), dtype=np.bool_) for team, batch in obs.items()}


def _transition(
    team: str,
    obs: Mapping[str, np.ndarray],
    actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray],
    result: StepResult,
    *,
    active: np.ndarray,
) -> Transition:
    """One team's batch-first step; agents done only by truncation are not terminated."""
    return Transition(
        obs=obs[team],
        actions=actions[team],
        reward=result.reward[team],
        next_obs=result.obs[team],
        terminated=result.done[team] & active & (not result.truncated),
        active=active,
        truncated=result.truncated,
    )


def _mean_score(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], spec: EvalSpec) -> float:
    """A greedy evaluation of the first learning team against the run's opposition."""
    return float(np.mean(evaluate(make_env, agents, spec).scores))


def _progress(settings: RunSettings, step: int, games: int, elapsed_s: float) -> str:
    return (
        f"{settings.name} s{settings.seed}: step {step}/{settings.steps}, "
        f"{games} games, {elapsed_s:.0f} s"
    )


def _save_results(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    settings: RunSettings,
    writer: RunWriter,
) -> None:
    """Each learner's checkpoint, and `RECORDED_GAMES` recorded greedy games."""
    learners = learning_teams(settings)
    for team in learners:
        writer.save_checkpoint(agents[team], team=team)
    spec = EvalSpec(
        settings.name,
        settings.seed,
        team=learners[0],
        n_games=RECORDED_GAMES,
        record=RECORDED_GAMES,
    )
    writer.save_games(evaluate(make_env, agents, spec).games)
"""The environment and the agents a run's settings name (PL-01 steps 2 and 7)."""

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Literal

from rllab.agents import DQNAgent, TabularAgent
from rllab.baselines import RandomPlayer, ScriptedPlayer
from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, Env, RunSettings, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs import Corridor, Snake


def make_env(settings: RunSettings) -> Callable[[], Env]:
    """A zero-argument factory of the settings' environment."""
    rewards = settings.rewards
    if settings.env == "snake":
        view: Literal["compact", "grid"] = "grid" if settings.view == "grid" else "compact"
        return lambda: Snake(view=view, rewards=rewards)
    if settings.env == "corridor":
        return lambda: Corridor(rewards=rewards)
    raise ValueError(f"env {settings.env!r}: not built in this milestone")


def make_agent(settings: RunSettings, env: Env) -> dict[str, Agent]:
    """One agent per team of `env`, sized from the team's `TeamSpec`."""
    return {SOLO: _solo_agent(settings, env.teams[SOLO])}


def _solo_agent(settings: RunSettings, team: TeamSpec) -> Agent:
    """The single team's player: a learner or a baseline, with the settings' ablation."""
    seed = settings.seed
    if settings.agent == "tabular":
        return TabularAgent(
            team.actions.n,
            settings.params,
            settings.exploration,
            seed=seed,
            explore_enabled=settings.ablation != "no_explore",
        )
    if settings.agent == "dqn":
        ablation = settings.ablation if settings.ablation != "no_explore" else "none"
        return DQNAgent(team, settings.params, settings.exploration, seed=seed, ablation=ablation)
    if settings.agent == "random":
        return RandomPlayer(team.actions.n, seed=seed)
    if settings.agent == "scripted":
        return ScriptedPlayer()
    raise ValueError(f"agent {settings.agent!r}: not built from settings in this milestone")

"""A 1-D corridor with the goal at its far end (CORE-03)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec

_DEFAULT_REWARDS = Rewards()
_AGENT_CELL = 1
_GOAL_CELL = 3


class Corridor(Env):
    """One team, `SOLO`, of one agent; actions 0 left, 1 right; one-hot position view."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid"]

    def __init__(
        self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = _DEFAULT_REWARDS
    ) -> None:
        if length < 2:
            raise ValueError(f"corridor length must be at least 2, got {length}")
        if max_steps < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"max_steps must be at least 1, got {max_steps}")
        self.teams = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (length,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=2))}
        self.board = "grid"
        self._length = length
        self._max_steps = max_steps
        self._rewards = rewards
        self._position = 0
        self._steps = 0
        self._score = 0.0
        self._over = True

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start at cell 0; the corridor has no randomness, so `seed` changes nothing."""
        self._position = 0
        self._steps = 0
        self._score = 0.0
        self._over = False
        return self._obs(), self._info()

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        if self._over:
            raise RuntimeError("step called after the game is over; call reset first")
        move = 1 if _read_action(actions) == 1 else -1
        self._position = min(max(self._position + move, 0), self._length - 1)
        self._steps += 1
        terminated = self._position == self._length - 1
        truncated = not terminated and self._steps >= self._max_steps
        reward = self._rewards.food if terminated else 0.0
        self._score += reward
        self._over = terminated or truncated
        return StepResult(
            obs=self._obs(),
            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], dtype=np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([terminated], dtype=np.bool_)},
            terminated=terminated,
            truncated=truncated,
            info=self._info(),
        )

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """A `(1, length)` grid: the goal in the last cell, the agent drawn over it."""
        grid = np.zeros((1, self._length), dtype=np.int8)
        grid[0, -1] = _GOAL_CELL
        grid[0, self._position] = _AGENT_CELL
        return grid

    def _obs(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        one_hot = np.zeros((1, self._length), dtype=np.float32)
        one_hot[0, self._position] = 1.0
        return {SOLO: one_hot}

    def _info(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        return {"steps": float(self._steps), f"score:{SOLO}": self._score, "score": self._score}


def _read_action(actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> int:
    """The one agent's action, checked: team present, shape `(1,)`, finite, 0 or 1."""
    if SOLO not in actions:
        raise ValueError(f"actions has no entry for team {SOLO!r}")
    action = np.asarray(actions[SOLO])
    if action.shape != (1,):
        raise ValueError(f"action for team {SOLO!r} must have shape (1,), got {action.shape}")
    value = float(action[0])
    if value not in (0.0, 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"action for team {SOLO!r} must be 0 (left) or 1 (right), got {value}")
    return int(value)
"""PL-04 end to end: `python -m rllab.lab report reports/smoke.toml --smoke`."""

import shutil
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from rllab.lab.cli import main
from rllab.lab.pipelines import pl04_report
from rllab.lab.types import CurveRuns
from rllab.runs import RunRecord

pytestmark = pytest.mark.unit

SMOKE = Path(__file__).parents[3] / "reports" / "smoke.toml"
PNG_MAGIC = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"


def test_smoke_report_writes_report_and_both_charts(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    spec = tmp_path / "smoke.toml"
    shutil.copy(SMOKE, spec)

    assert main(["report", str(spec), "--smoke"]) == 0

    report = (tmp_path / "report.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "- scripted: mean" in report
    assert "- random: mean" in report
    assert "scripted greater than random: PASSED" in report
    for chart in ("ladder.png", "ablations.png"):
        assert (tmp_path / chart).read_bytes().startswith(PNG_MAGIC)


def test_smoke_report_with_unknown_player_exits_2(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    spec = tmp_path / "smoke.toml"
    text = SMOKE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    spec.write_text(text.replace('b = "random"', 'b = "nobody"'), encoding="utf-8")

    assert main(["report", str(spec), "--smoke"]) == 2
    assert not (tmp_path / "report.md").exists()


def test_run_report_draws_ladder_and_ablation_groups_to_their_own_charts(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    ladder: dict[str, tuple[RunRecord, ...]] = {"dqn-compact": ()}
    ablations: dict[str, tuple[RunRecord, ...]] = {"dqn-no-memory": (), "dqn-compact": ()}
    drawn: dict[str, list[str]] = {}

    def plot_curves(groups: dict[str, object], **kwargs: object) -> None:
        out = kwargs["out"]
        assert isinstance(out, Path)
        drawn[out.name] = list(groups)
        assert kwargs["baselines"] == ("scripted", "random")

    monkeypatch.setattr(pl04_report, "score_players", lambda targets: {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        pl04_report, "open_curve_runs", lambda targets: CurveRuns(ladder, ablations)
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(pl04_report, "run_claims", lambda scores, specs, out: ["claim"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(pl04_report, "plot_curves", plot_curves)

    assert pl04_report.run_report(SMOKE, out=tmp_path) == ["claim"]
    assert drawn == {
        "ladder.png": ["dqn-compact"],
        "ablations.png": ["dqn-no-memory", "dqn-compact"],
    }

**Skill routing:** `compare` owns every significance claim. It hands the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill two score sets and a direction (for example "dqn-compact greater than random", "ppo-1 greater than reinforce-1" or "rung-3 prey greater than rung-2 prey against the prey panel"). The skill chooses the test statistic, the null distribution and the multiple-comparison correction, and `compare` records the skill's verdict as a `compare.Claim`. `compare` adds no test procedure of its own. The 90%-of-mean check in REF-02 is plain arithmetic, not a test.

## Interfaces
See [packages/rllab/contract.md](packages/rllab/contract.md).

## Data
- All data comes from the lab's own environments (DATA-02). No downloads.
- **Seeds (DATA-01):** a run's training seed `s` must be below 1,000,000. Its evaluation seeds are `1_000_000 + 1000·s + i`, a disjoint range. `evaluate.eval_seeds` asserts that none of them overlaps the run's training seed stream.
- **Ladder runs:** 5 training seeds (0–4) for each rung. Snake: `tabular-compact`, `dqn-compact`, `dqn-grid`, `reference-compact` and the three ablations. Fish tank: `reinforce-1`, `ppo-1`, `ppo-school`, `ppo-school-limited` and `reference-ppo-1`. Predator-prey: `pp-r1-prey`, `pp-r1-predator`, `pp-r2`, `pp-r3` and `reference-pp-predator`; the panels (SELF-07) are each team's scripted and random players plus the other team's rung-1 policy. Each environment's `random` and `scripted` players are evaluated on the same evaluation seeds. Run settings live in `settings/`.

## Cross-cutting concerns
- **Reproducibility (RUN-03):** one seed feeds a single `np.random.Generator` and `torch.manual_seed`, passed down explicitly. PyTorch runs with one thread and deterministic algorithms (AD-08). No global random state.
- **Timing (QR-01–QR-04, QR-07):** predator-prey runs count both learning teams' updates inside one run's 60 minutes. Each run's progress lines and `curve.csv` carry elapsed seconds, so the limits are read from the runs themselves. A timing test trains each agent for 10,000 steps and extrapolates to its full step budget.
- **Laptop only (QR-06):** PyTorch is pinned to CPU. Nothing selects a GPU. Stable-Baselines3 is given `device="cpu"`, because its default `"auto"` would otherwise pick any GPU it finds.
- **Cloud cap (QR-05):** a rung may go to rented hardware only after its laptop timing test fails QR-03 or QR-07. Spending is tracked by hand against $5 per stage.
- **From scratch (AGENT-06):** import-linter keeps `selfplay` below `train` and above `evaluate`, and forbids `agents`, `core`, `envs`, `baselines`, `selfplay` and `train` from importing `stable_baselines3` or `gymnasium`.
- **Errors:** bad settings raise `ValueError` naming the key. Stepping a finished game raises `RuntimeError`. A missing or broken run folder raises `FileNotFoundError` naming the run.
- **Testing:** pytest, with rule tests per Snake and tank requirement against hand-built states (`Snake.reset_to`, `FishTank.reset_to`, `PredatorPrey.reset_to`).

1	"""Tested claims over pooled scores, and the report (PL-04 step 4)."""
2	
3	import math
4	from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
5	from pathlib import Path
6	
7	import numpy as np
8	
9	from rllab.compare.types import Claim, ClaimSpec
10	from rllab.evaluate import ScoreSet
11	from rllab.viz import Chart
12	
13	PERMUTATIONS = 9999
14	PERMUTATION_BATCH = 1000
15	PERMUTATION_SEED = 0
16	
17	
18	def run_claims(
19	    scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
20	    specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec],
21	    *,
22	    alpha: float = 0.05,
23	    charts: Sequence[Chart] = (),
24	    out: Path,
25	) -> list[Claim]:
26	    """Test every claim with one correction over all of them, and write `out/report.md`."""
27	    pooled = pool_scores(scores)
28	    check_players(specs, pooled)
29	    p_values = [permutation_p_value(*tested_pair(spec, pooled)) for spec in specs]
30	    corrected = holm(p_values)
31	    method = (
32	        "statistic: difference in pooled mean score (one-sided); "
33	        f"null: permutation of player labels over pooled games ({PERMUTATIONS} shuffles); "
34	        f"correction: Holm over {len(specs)} claim(s)"
35	    )
36	    claims = [
37	        Claim(
38	            spec=spec,
39	            mean_a=float(pooled[spec.a].mean()),
40	            mean_b=float(pooled[spec.b].mean()),
41	            p_value=p,
42	            p_corrected=p_corr,
43	            passed=claim_passed(spec, pooled, p_corr, alpha),
44	            method=method,
45	        )
46	        for spec, p, p_corr in zip(specs, p_values, corrected, strict=True)
47	    ]
48	    write_report(out / "report.md", pooled, claims, charts)
49	    return claims
50	
51	
52	def pool_scores(scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]]) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
53	    """Each player's games pooled across its training seeds."""
54	    return {
55	        player: np.concatenate([s.scores for s in sets]).astype(np.float64)
56	        for player, sets in scores.items()
57	    }
58	
59	
60	def check_players(specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec], pooled: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> None:
61	    """Raise `ValueError` naming any player a spec uses that has no scores."""
62	    for spec in specs:
63	        missing = [p for p in (spec.a, spec.b) if p not in pooled]
64	        if missing:
65	            raise ValueError(f"claim {spec.name!r} names players without scores: {missing}")
66	
67	
68	def tested_pair(spec: ClaimSpec, pooled: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
69	    """The (x, y) whose H1 is mean(x) > mean(y): a over b, or b over a for "not worse"."""
70	    if spec.direction == "greater":
71	        return pooled[spec.a], pooled[spec.b]
72	    return pooled[spec.b], pooled[spec.a]
73	
74	
75	def permutation_p_value(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> float:
76	    """One-sided p-value of mean(x) - mean(y) under shuffled labels, (hits + 1) / (n + 1)."""
77	    rng = np.random.default_rng(PERMUTATION_SEED)
78	    both = np.concatenate([x, y])
79	    observed = x.mean() - y.mean()
80	    hits = 0
81	    for start in range(0, PERMUTATIONS, PERMUTATION_BATCH):
82	        n = min(PERMUTATION_BATCH, PERMUTATIONS - start)
83	        shuffled = rng.permuted(np.tile(both, (n, 1)), axis=1)
84	        diffs = shuffled[:, : len(x)].mean(axis=1) - shuffled[:, len(x) :].mean(axis=1)
85	        hits += int(np.count_nonzero(diffs >= observed))
86	    return (hits + 1) / (PERMUTATIONS + 1)
87	
88	
89	def holm(p_values: Sequence[float]) -> list[float]:
90	    """Holm step-down adjusted p-values, in input order."""
91	    m = len(p_values)
92	    adjusted = [0.0] * m
93	    running = 0.0
94	    for rank, i in enumerate(sorted(range(m), key=lambda k: p_values[k])):
95	        running = max(running, min(1.0, (m - rank) * p_values[i]))
96	        adjusted[i] = running
97	    return adjusted
98	
99	
100	def claim_passed(
101	    spec: ClaimSpec, pooled: Mapping[str, np.ndarray], p_corrected: float, alpha: float
102	) -> bool:
103	    """`greater`: significant; `not_worse`: "a worse" not significant and the ratio bar holds."""
104	    significant = p_corrected <= alpha
105	    if spec.direction == "greater":
106	        return significant
107	    if spec.min_ratio is None:
108	        return not significant
109	    return not significant and ratio_holds(
110	        float(pooled[spec.a].mean()), float(pooled[spec.b].mean()), spec.min_ratio
111	    )
112	
113	
114	def ratio_holds(mean_a: float, mean_b: float, min_ratio: float) -> bool:
115	    """`mean_a >= min_ratio * mean_b`, inclusive up to float rounding."""
116	    bar = min_ratio * mean_b
117	    return mean_a >= bar or math.isclose(mean_a, bar)
118	
119	
120	def write_report(
121	    path: Path, pooled: Mapping[str, np.ndarray], claims: Sequence[Claim], charts: Sequence[Chart]
122	) -> None:
123	    """Write each player's mean and spread, each claim, and a link to each cross-play chart."""
124	    lines = ["# Report", "", "## Players", ""]
125	    lines += [
126	        f"- {player}: mean {s.mean():.3f}, std {s.std():.3f}, "
127	        f"min {s.min():.3f}, max {s.max():.3f} over {s.size} games"
128	        for player, s in pooled.items()
129	    ]
130	    lines += ["", "## Claims", ""]
131	    lines += [claim_line(claim) for claim in claims]
132	    if claims:
133	        lines += ["", f"Method: {claims[0].method}"]
134	    if charts:
135	        lines += ["", "## Cross-play", ""]
136	        lines += [f"- [{chart.path.name}]({chart.path})" for chart in charts]
137	    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

1	"""Learning curves with baseline lines (PL-04 step 5)."""
2	
3	from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
4	from pathlib import Path
5	
6	import numpy as np
7	from matplotlib.figure import Figure
8	
9	from rllab.evaluate import ScoreSet
10	from rllab.runs import RunRecord
11	from rllab.viz.types import Chart
12	
13	
14	def plot_curves(
15	    groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]],
16	    *,
17	    scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
18	    baselines: Sequence[str],
19	    out: Path,
20	) -> Chart:
21	    """One mean line with a min-max band per group, one horizontal line per baseline."""
22	    missing = [name for name in baselines if name not in scores]
23	    if missing:
24	        raise ValueError(f"no scores for baselines {missing}")
25	    figure = Figure(figsize=(8, 5))
26	    axes = figure.add_subplot()
27	    for name, runs in groups.items():
28	        steps, mean, low, high = _band(runs)
29	        (line,) = axes.plot(steps, mean, label=name)
30	        axes.fill_between(steps, low, high, color=line.get_color(), alpha=0.2)
31	    for i, name in enumerate(baselines):
32	        axes.axhline(_pooled_mean(scores[name]), color=f"C{len(groups) + i}", ls="--", label=name)
33	    axes.set_xlabel("training step")
34	    axes.set_ylabel("mean score")
35	    axes.legend()
36	    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
37	    figure.savefig(out, format="png")
38	    return Chart(path=out, groups=tuple(groups), baselines=tuple(baselines))
39	
40	
41	def _band(
42	    runs: Sequence[RunRecord],
43	) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
44	    """Steps every run evaluated at, and the mean, min and max score over the runs there."""
45	    common = set.intersection(*({point.step for point in run.curve} for run in runs))
46	    steps = np.array(sorted(common), dtype=np.int64)
47	    table = np.array(
48	        [[point.mean_score for point in run.curve if point.step in common] for run in runs],
49	        dtype=np.float64,
50	    ).reshape(len(runs), len(steps))
51	    return steps, table.mean(axis=0), table.min(axis=0), table.max(axis=0)
52	
53	
54	def _pooled_mean(sets: Sequence[ScoreSet]) -> float:
55	    """The mean over every game of every seed."""
56	    return float(np.concatenate([s.scores for s in sets]).mean())
