1	"""Snake on a square grid, one agent, compact or grid view (SNAKE-01 to SNAKE-09)."""
2	
3	from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
4	from itertools import pairwise
5	from typing import Literal
6	
7	import numpy as np
8	
9	from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec
10	
11	_DEFAULT_REWARDS = Rewards()
12	_HEADINGS = ("up", "right", "down", "left")
13	"""Clockwise, so a left turn is one index back and a right turn one index on."""
14	_MOVES = ((-1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0), (0, -1))
15	_TURNS = (0, -1, 1)
16	"""Heading change per action: 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right."""
17	_STARVE_PER_CELL = 100
18	"""The game is cut short after this many steps without food per cell of length (SNAKE-05)."""
19	_EMPTY, _HEAD, _BODY, _FOOD = 0, 1, 2, 3
20	
21	Cell = tuple[int, int]
22	
23	
24	class Snake(Env):
25	    """One team, `SOLO`, of one snake; actions 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right."""
26	
27	    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
28	    board: Literal["grid"]
29	
30	    def __init__(
31	        self,
32	        *,
33	        view: Literal["compact", "grid"],
34	        rewards: Rewards = _DEFAULT_REWARDS,
35	        size: int = 10,
36	    ) -> None:
37	        obs_shape = (11,) if view == "compact" else (3, size, size)
38	        self.teams = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, obs_shape, ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))}
39	        self.board = "grid"
40	        self._view = view
41	        self._rewards = rewards
42	        self._size = size
43	        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
44	        self._body: list[Cell] = []
45	        self._heading = 1
46	        self._food: Cell | None = None
47	        self._steps = 0
48	        self._hungry = 0
49	        self._score = 0
50	        self._over = True
51	
52	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
53	        """A length-3 snake in the middle heading right, food on a seeded free cell."""
54	        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
55	        row, col = self._size // 2, self._size // 2
56	        self._start([(row, col), (row, col - 1), (row, col - 2)], heading=1)
57	        self._food = self._place_food()
58	        return {SOLO: self._observe()}, self._info()
59	
60	    def reset_to(
61	        self,
62	        body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]],
63	        heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"],
64	        food: tuple[int, int],
65	    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, dict[str, int]]:
66	        """Start a game from a given board: cells `(row, col)`, body head first."""
67	        cells = [(int(r), int(c)) for r, c in body]
68	        food_cell = (int(food[0]), int(food[1]))
69	        self._check_board(cells, food_cell)
70	        self._start(cells, heading=_HEADINGS.index(heading))
71	        self._food = food_cell
72	        return self._observe(), {"steps": 0, f"score:{SOLO}": 0, "score": 0}
73	
74	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
75	        if self._over:
76	            raise RuntimeError("step called while no game is running")
77	        action = self._check_action(actions)
78	        self._steps += 1
79	        self._heading = (self._heading + _TURNS[action]) % 4
80	        head = _ahead(self._body[0], self._heading)
81	        terminated, truncated, reward = self._move(head)
82	        self._over = terminated or truncated
83	        return StepResult(
84	            obs={SOLO: self._observe()},
85	            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], dtype=np.float32)},
86	            done={SOLO: np.array([terminated], dtype=np.bool_)},
87	            terminated=terminated,
88	            truncated=truncated,
89	            info=self._info(),
90	        )
91	
92	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
93	        grid = np.full((self._size, self._size), _EMPTY, dtype=np.int8)
94	        if self._food is not None:
95	            grid[self._food] = _FOOD
96	        for cell in self._body[1:]:
97	            grid[cell] = _BODY
98	        if self._body:
99	            grid[self._body[0]] = _HEAD
100	        return grid
101	
102	    def _start(self, body: list[Cell], *, heading: int) -> None:
103	        self._body = body
104	        self._heading = heading
105	        self._steps = 0
106	        self._hungry = 0
107	        self._score = 0
108	        self._over = False
109	
110	    def _move(self, head: Cell) -> tuple[bool, bool, float]:
111	        """Move the head to `head`: return `(terminated, truncated, reward)`."""
112	        if self._blocked(head):
113	            return True, False, self._rewards.death
114	        self._body.insert(0, head)
115	        if head == self._food:
116	            self._score += 1
117	            self._hungry = 0
118	            if len(self._body) == self._size * self._size:
119	                self._food = None
120	                return True, False, self._rewards.food
121	            self._food = self._place_food()
122	            return False, False, self._rewards.food
123	        self._body.pop()
124	        self._hungry += 1
125	        if self._hungry >= _STARVE_PER_CELL * len(self._body):
126	            return False, True, self._rewards.death
127	        return False, False, self._rewards.step
128	
129	    def _blocked(self, cell: Cell) -> bool:
130	        """Whether moving the head into `cell` this step is a collision; the tail is leaving."""
131	        return not self._inside(cell) or cell in self._body[:-1]
132	
133	    def _place_food(self) -> Cell:
134	        free = np.argwhere(self.frame() == _EMPTY)
135	        row, col = free[self._rng.integers(len(free))]
136	        return int(row), int(col)
137	
138	    def _observe(self) -> np.ndarray:
139	        """The snake's observation, batch-first over its one agent."""
140	        if self._view == "compact":
141	            return self._compact()[np.newaxis]
142	        grid = self.frame()
143	        channels = np.stack([grid == _HEAD, grid == _BODY, grid == _FOOD])
144	        return channels.astype(np.float32)[np.newaxis]
145	
146	    def _compact(self) -> np.ndarray:
147	        """The 11 flags laid out as `COMPACT_FIELDS`."""
148	        head = self._body[0]
149	        danger = [_ahead(head, (self._heading + turn) % 4) for turn in _TURNS]
150	        food_row, food_col = self._food if self._food is not None else head
151	        flags = [
152	            *(self._blocked(cell) for cell in danger),
153	            *(self._heading == h for h in range(4)),
154	            food_col < head[1],
155	            food_col > head[1],
156	            food_row < head[0],
157	            food_row > head[0],
158	        ]
159	        return np.array(flags, dtype=np.float32)
160	
161	    def _info(self) -> dict[str, float]:
162	        score = float(self._score)
163	        return {"steps": float(self._steps), f"score:{SOLO}": score, "score": score}
164	
165	    def _check_board(self, body: list[Cell], food: Cell) -> None:
166	        """`ValueError` unless the body is contiguous on the grid and the food is off it."""
167	        if not body or any(not self._inside(cell) for cell in body):
168	            raise ValueError(f"snake body {body} leaves the {self._size}x{self._size} grid")
169	        if len(set(body)) != len(body) or any(_apart(a, b) != 1 for a, b in pairwise(body)):
170	            raise ValueError(f"snake body {body} is not contiguous")
171	        if not self._inside(food) or food in body:
172	            raise ValueError(f"food {food} is on the snake or off the grid")
173	
174	    def _inside(self, cell: Cell) -> bool:
175	        return 0 <= cell[0] < self._size and 0 <= cell[1] < self._size
176	
177	    def _check_action(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> int:
178	        """The one action, or `ValueError` for a missing team, a wrong shape or range."""
179	        if SOLO not in actions:
180	            raise ValueError(f"no actions for team {SOLO!r}")
181	        action = np.asarray(actions[SOLO])
182	        if action.shape != (1,):
183	            raise ValueError(f"team {SOLO!r}: actions shaped {action.shape}, expected (1,)")
184	        if not np.all(np.isfinite(action)):
185	            raise ValueError(f"team {SOLO!r}: non-finite action")
186	        value = action[0]
187	        if value != int(value) or not 0 <= value < len(_TURNS):
188	            raise ValueError(f"team {SOLO!r}: action {value} outside 0..{len(_TURNS) - 1}")
189	        return int(value)
190	
191	
192	def _ahead(cell: Cell, heading: int) -> Cell:
193	    d_row, d_col = _MOVES[heading]
194	    return cell[0] + d_row, cell[1] + d_col
195	
196	
197	def _apart(a: Cell, b: Cell) -> int:
198	    return abs(a[0] - b[0]) + abs(a[1] - b[1])

1	"""The neural value learner (AGENT-03 to AGENT-05, ABL-01, ABL-02)."""
2	
3	import copy
4	from pathlib import Path
5	from typing import Literal
6	
7	import numpy as np
8	import torch
9	from torch import nn
10	
11	from rllab.agents.dqn.networks import build_net
12	from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, TeamSpec, Transition
13	
14	GRAD_CLIP = 10.0
15	"""Largest gradient norm per update."""
16	
17	
18	def exploration_rate(exploration: Exploration, steps: int) -> float:
19	    """The rate after `steps` steps: linear from `start` to `end` at `decay_steps`, then flat."""
20	    if exploration.decay_steps <= 0:
21	        return exploration.end
22	    fraction = min(1.0, steps / exploration.decay_steps)
23	    return exploration.start + (exploration.end - exploration.start) * fraction
24	
25	
26	def _check_team(team: TeamSpec, params: AgentParams) -> None:
27	    if team.n_agents != 1:
28	        raise ValueError(f"DQNAgent plays one agent, got n_agents={team.n_agents}")
29	    if team.actions.kind != "discrete":
30	        raise ValueError(f"DQNAgent needs discrete actions, got {team.actions.kind!r}")
31	    if params.net == "cnn" and len(team.obs_shape) != 3:
32	        raise ValueError(f'net="cnn" needs a 3-D obs_shape, got {team.obs_shape}')
33	
34	
35	class DQNAgent(Agent):
36	    """A value network sized from `team`, with a replay memory and a target copy.
37	
38	    `ablation="no_memory"` updates on the latest transition only (ABL-01); `"no_target"` makes
39	    the target the online network itself, so targets come from the online network (ABL-02).
40	    """
41	
42	    def __init__(
43	        self,
44	        team: TeamSpec,
45	        params: AgentParams,
46	        exploration: Exploration,
47	        *,
48	        seed: int,
49	        ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none",
50	    ) -> None:
51	        _check_team(team, params)
52	        torch.set_num_threads(1)
53	        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
54	        self._params = params
55	        self._exploration = exploration
56	        self._n_actions = team.actions.n
57	        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
58	        with torch.random.fork_rng():
59	            torch.manual_seed(seed)
60	            self._online = build_net(team.obs_shape, team.actions.n, params)
61	        self._no_memory = ablation == "no_memory"
62	        self._target = self._online if ablation == "no_target" else copy.deepcopy(self._online)
63	        self._optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self._online.parameters(), lr=params.lr)
64	        size = params.memory_size
65	        self._obs = np.zeros((size, *team.obs_shape), dtype=np.float32)
66	        self._next_obs = np.zeros((size, *team.obs_shape), dtype=np.float32)
67	        self._actions = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.int64)
68	        self._rewards = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float32)
69	        self._terminated = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float32)
70	        self._stored = 0
71	        self._updates = 0
72	
73	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
74	        """Greedy over the online network's values; with `explore`, random at the current rate."""
75	        if explore and self._rng.random() < exploration_rate(self._exploration, self._stored):
76	            return self._rng.integers(0, self._n_actions, size=1, dtype=np.int64)
77	        with torch.no_grad():
78	            values: torch.Tensor = self._online(torch.as_tensor(obs, dtype=torch.float32))
79	        return values.argmax(dim=1).numpy().astype(np.int64)
80	
81	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
82	        """Remember the step; from `learn_start` on, one update on a random batch of memory.
83	
84	        With `no_memory` the batch is the latest transition alone.
85	        """
86	        if not t.active[0]:
87	            return
88	        self._remember(t)
89	        if self._stored >= self._params.learn_start:
90	            self._update(self._batch_rows())
91	
92	    def online_state(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
93	        """A detached copy of the online network's state dict."""
94	        return _detached(self._online)
95	
96	    def target_state(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
97	        """A detached copy of the target network's state dict."""
98	        return _detached(self._target)
99	
100	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
101	        """Write both networks' state dicts, under `online` and `target`."""
102	        torch.save({"online": self.online_state(), "target": self.target_state()}, path)
103	
104	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
105	        """Read a checkpoint written by `save` into the online and target networks."""
106	        checkpoint = torch.load(path, weights_only=True)
107	        self._online.load_state_dict(checkpoint["online"])
108	        self._target.load_state_dict(checkpoint["target"])
109	
110	    def _batch_rows(self) -> np.ndarray:
111	        if self._no_memory:
112	            return np.array([(self._stored - 1) % self._params.memory_size])
113	        filled = min(self._stored, self._params.memory_size)
114	        return self._rng.integers(0, filled, size=self._params.batch_size)
115	
116	    def _remember(self, t: Transition) -> None:
117	        slot = self._stored % self._params.memory_size
118	        self._obs[slot] = t.obs[0]
119	        self._next_obs[slot] = t.next_obs[0]
120	        self._actions[slot] = t.actions[0]
121	        self._rewards[slot] = t.reward[0]
122	        self._terminated[slot] = t.terminated[0]
123	        self._stored += 1
124	
125	    def _update(self, rows: np.ndarray) -> None:
126	        obs = torch.as_tensor(self._obs[rows])
127	        next_obs = torch.as_tensor(self._next_obs[rows])
128	        actions = torch.as_tensor(self._actions[rows])
129	        rewards = torch.as_tensor(self._rewards[rows])
130	        terminated = torch.as_tensor(self._terminated[rows])
131	        with torch.no_grad():
132	            next_values: torch.Tensor = self._target(next_obs)
133	            targets = (
134	                rewards + self._params.gamma * (1.0 - terminated) * next_values.max(dim=1).values
135	            )
136	        values: torch.Tensor = self._online(obs)
137	        chosen = values.gather(1, actions.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)
138	        loss = nn.functional.smooth_l1_loss(chosen, targets)
139	        self._optimizer.zero_grad()
140	        torch.autograd.backward(loss)
141	        nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self._online.parameters(), GRAD_CLIP)
142	        self._optimizer.step()
143	        self._updates += 1
144	        if self._updates % self._params.target_every == 0:
145	            self._target.load_state_dict(self._online.state_dict())
146	
147	
148	def _detached(net: nn.Module) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
149	    return {name: tensor.detach().clone() for name, tensor in net.state_dict().items()}
150

1	"""The scripted baseline on the compact view (BASE-02)."""
2	
3	from pathlib import Path
4	
5	import numpy as np
6	
7	from rllab.core import Agent, Transition
8	from rllab.envs import COMPACT_FIELDS
9	
10	# Relative moves in tie order: action 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right.
11	_MOVES = (0, 1, 2)
12	# Compass directions clockwise, so a left turn is -1 and a right turn +1.
13	_HEADING = [COMPACT_FIELDS.index(f"head_{d}") for d in ("up", "right", "down", "left")]
14	_FOOD = [COMPACT_FIELDS.index(f"food_{d}") for d in ("up", "right", "down", "left")]
15	_DANGER = [COMPACT_FIELDS.index(f"danger_{m}") for m in ("straight", "left", "right")]
16	_TURN = (0, -1, 1)
17	
18	
19	class ScriptedPlayer(Agent):
20	    """Toward food if safe, else any safe move, else straight; ties straight, left, right."""
21	
22	    def __init__(self) -> None:
23	        """The player reads only the compact view; its choice depends on nothing else."""
24	        self.obs_shape = (len(COMPACT_FIELDS),)
25	
26	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
27	        """One move per agent from its compact view; `explore` changes nothing."""
28	        if obs.ndim != 2 or obs.shape[1:] != self.obs_shape:
29	            raise ValueError(f"each observation must be shape (11,), got {obs.shape[1:]}")
30	        return np.asarray([_choose(row) for row in obs], dtype=np.int64)
31	
32	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
33	        """A scripted player does not learn: the transition is dropped."""
34	        del t
35	
36	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
37	        """Nothing to save: the script is the whole player."""
38	        del path
39	
40	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
41	        """Nothing to load: the script is the whole player."""
42	        del path
43	
44	
45	def _choose(view: np.ndarray) -> int:
46	    """The first safe move toward food, else the first safe move, else straight."""
47	    heading = int(np.argmax(view[_HEADING]))
48	    safe = [m for m in _MOVES if view[_DANGER[m]] == 0]
49	    toward = [m for m in safe if view[_FOOD[(heading + _TURN[m]) % 4]] == 1]
50	    return (toward or safe or [0])[0]

1	"""The table-based value learner (AGENT-01, AGENT-02, ABL-03)."""
2	
3	from pathlib import Path
4	
5	import numpy as np
6	
7	from rllab.agents.dqn.agent import exploration_rate
8	from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Transition
9	
10	
11	def _key(obs: np.ndarray) -> bytes:
12	    """The table key of a `(1, *obs_shape)` observation."""
13	    if obs.shape[0] != 1:
14	        raise ValueError(f"TabularAgent takes a batch of 1, got {obs.shape[0]}")
15	    return np.ascontiguousarray(obs[0]).tobytes()
16	
17	
18	class TabularAgent(Agent):
19	    """One value per (observation bytes, action), starting at 0; batches of one agent."""
20	
21	    def __init__(
22	        self,
23	        n_actions: int,
24	        params: AgentParams,
25	        exploration: Exploration,
26	        *,
27	        seed: int,
28	        explore_enabled: bool = True,
29	    ) -> None:
30	        self._n_actions = n_actions
31	        self._params = params
32	        self._exploration = exploration
33	        self._explore_enabled = explore_enabled
34	        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
35	        self._table: dict[bytes, np.ndarray] = {}
36	        self._learns = 0
37	
38	    def values(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
39	        """The `(n_actions,)` values of a `(1, *obs_shape)` observation; zeros when unseen."""
40	        row = self._table.get(_key(obs))
41	        return np.zeros(self._n_actions) if row is None else row.copy()
42	
43	    def epsilon(self, step: int) -> float:
44	        """The exploration rate after `step` `learn` calls."""
45	        if step < 0:
46	            raise ValueError(f"step must be non-negative, got {step}")
47	        return exploration_rate(self._exploration, step)
48	
49	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
50	        """The best-valued action; with `explore` (unless disabled), random at the current rate."""
51	        explores = explore and self._explore_enabled
52	        if explores and self._rng.random() < self.epsilon(self._learns):
53	            return self._rng.integers(0, self._n_actions, size=1, dtype=np.int64)
54	        return np.array([np.argmax(self.values(obs))], dtype=np.int64)
55	
56	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
57	        """Move Q(obs, action) toward `reward + gamma * max next value`, not bootstrapping at an end."""
58	        key = _key(t.obs)
59	        row = self._table.setdefault(key, np.zeros(self._n_actions))
60	        bootstrap = 0.0 if bool(t.terminated[0]) else float(np.max(self.values(t.next_obs)))
61	        target = float(t.reward[0]) + self._params.gamma * bootstrap
62	        action = int(t.actions[0])
63	        row[action] += self._params.lr * (target - row[action])
64	        self._learns += 1
65	
66	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
67	        """The table as NPZ: one uint8 row of key bytes and one value row per observation."""
68	        keys = [np.frombuffer(key, dtype=np.uint8) for key in self._table]
69	        np.savez(
70	            path,
71	            keys=np.stack(keys) if keys else np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=np.uint8),
72	            values=np.stack(list(self._table.values())) if keys else np.zeros((0, self._n_actions)),
73	            learns=np.array(self._learns),
74	        )
75	
76	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
77	        """Replace the table and the learn count with those saved at `path`."""
78	        with np.load(path) as archive:
79	            self._table = {
80	                key.tobytes(): row.copy()
81	                for key, row in zip(archive["keys"], archive["values"], strict=True)
82	            }
83	            self._learns = int(archive["learns"])
84

4	from pathlib import Path
5	from time import monotonic

7	
8	import numpy as np
9	from stable_baselines3 import DQN
10	from stable_baselines3.common.callbacks import BaseCallback

12	from rllab.core import SOLO, Env, RunSettings
13	from rllab.envs import Snake
14	from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, evaluate
15	from rllab.reference.player import ReferencePlayer
16	from rllab.reference.train.adapter import GymAdapter

18	from rllab.runs import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord, RunWriter, create_run
19	
20	RECORDED_GAMES = 5
21	
22	
23	def train_reference(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord:
24	    """Train the library agent on the settings' environment, with the same curve points."""
25	    make_env = _env_factory(settings)
26	    writer = create_run(settings, root=root)
27	    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = dqn_kwargs(settings)  # each value typed by the library's DQN
28	    model = DQN("MlpPolicy", GymAdapter(make_env()), **kwargs)
29	    player = _TrainingPlayer(model)
30	    try:
31	        model.learn(settings.steps, callback=_Curve(make_env, player, settings, writer))
32	        writer.save_checkpoint(player)
33	        writer.save_games(_recorded_games(make_env, player, settings))
34	    except KeyboardInterrupt:
35	        writer.finish("incomplete")
36	        raise
37	    return writer.finish("complete")
38	
39	
40	def _env_factory(settings: RunSettings) -> Callable[[], Env]:
41	    """Snake with the compact view: the one reference this milestone trains (AD-04)."""
42	    if settings.agent != "reference":
43	        raise ValueError(f"agent {settings.agent!r}: train_reference needs agent 'reference'")
44	    if settings.env != "snake" or settings.view != "compact":
45	        raise ValueError(f"the reference cannot train {settings.env}/{settings.view}")
46	    rewards = settings.rewards
47	
48	    def make_env() -> Env:
49	        return Snake(view="compact", rewards=rewards)
50	
51	    return make_env
52	
53	
54	class _TrainingPlayer(ReferencePlayer):
55	    """The model being trained, played greedily as an Agent."""
56	
57	    def __init__(self, model: DQN) -> None:
58	        self.model = model
59	
60	
61	class _Curve(BaseCallback):
62	    """A curve point at step 0, every `eval_every` steps and at the last step."""
63	
64	    def __init__(
65	        self,
66	        make_env: Callable[[], Env],
67	        player: _TrainingPlayer,
68	        settings: RunSettings,
69	        writer: RunWriter,
70	    ) -> None:
71	        super().__init__()
72	        self.make_env = make_env
73	        self.player = player
74	        self.settings = settings
75	        self.writer = writer
76	        self.spec = EvalSpec(settings.name, settings.seed, n_games=settings.eval_games)
77	        self.games = 0
78	        self.start = monotonic()
79	
80	    def _on_training_start(self) -> None:
81	        self.start = monotonic()
82	        self._add_point(0)
83	
84	    def _on_step(self) -> bool:
85	        self.games += int(np.sum(self.locals["dones"]))
86	        step = self.num_timesteps
87	        if step % self.settings.eval_every == 0 or step == self.settings.steps:
88	            self._add_point(step)
89	        return True
90	
91	    def _add_point(self, step: int) -> None:
92	        scores = evaluate(self.make_env, {SOLO: self.player}, self.spec).scores
93	        elapsed = monotonic() - self.start
94	        self.writer.add_point(CurvePoint(step, self.games, float(np.mean(scores)), elapsed))
95	
96	
97	def _recorded_games(
98	    make_env: Callable[[], Env], player: _TrainingPlayer, settings: RunSettings
99	) -> tuple[GameRecording, ...]:
100	    spec = EvalSpec(settings.name, settings.seed, n_games=RECORDED_GAMES, record=RECORDED_GAMES)
101	    return evaluate(make_env, {SOLO: player}, spec).games

4	
5	
6	def dqn_kwargs(settings: RunSettings) -> dict[str, object]:
7	    """Keyword arguments for `DQN("MlpPolicy", adapter, **kwargs)`, on the CPU (AD-04).
8	
9	    Huber loss is the library DQN's default, so no key sets it.
10	    """
11	    if settings.agent != "reference":
12	        raise ValueError(f"the library DQN maps reference settings, not agent {settings.agent!r}")
13	    if settings.env != "snake" or settings.view != "compact":
14	        raise ValueError(
15	            f"the library DQN runs on snake's compact view, not {settings.env}/{settings.view}"
16	        )
17	    params, exploration = settings.params, settings.exploration
18	    return {
19	        "learning_rate": params.lr,
20	        "buffer_size": params.memory_size,
21	        "learning_starts": params.learn_start,
22	        "batch_size": params.batch_size,
23	        "gamma": params.gamma,
24	        "train_freq": 1,
25	        "gradient_steps": 1,
26	        "target_update_interval": params.target_every,
27	        "exploration_fraction": min(1.0, exploration.decay_steps / settings.steps),
28	        "exploration_initial_eps": exploration.start,
29	        "exploration_final_eps": exploration.end,
30	        "max_grad_norm": 10,
31	        "policy_kwargs": {"net_arch": [params.hidden, params.hidden]},
32	        "seed": settings.seed,
33	        "device": "cpu",
34	    }

17	def _load(case: str) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
18	    folder = FIXTURES / case
19	    inputs = json.loads((folder / "input.json").read_text())
20	    expected = json.loads((folder / "expected.json").read_text())
21	    return inputs, expected
22	
23	
24	def _spec(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> ClaimSpec:
25	    return ClaimSpec(
26	        name=raw["name"],
27	        a=raw["a"],
28	        b=raw["b"],
29	        direction=raw["direction"],
30	        stretch=raw.get("stretch", False),
31	        min_ratio=raw.get("min_ratio"),
32	    )
33	
34	
35	def _poisson_scores(
36	    players: dict[str, float], seeds: int, games: int, seed: int
37	) -> dict[str, tuple[ScoreSet, ...]]:
38	    """Generator `poisson_scores`: per player, `seeds` ScoreSets of `games` Poisson draws."""
39	    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
40	    out: dict[str, tuple[ScoreSet, ...]] = {}
41	    for name, lam in players.items():
42	        out[name] = tuple(
43	            ScoreSet(
44	                player=name,
45	                train_seed=train_seed,
46	                scores=rng.poisson(lam, games).astype(np.float64),
47	                games=(),
48	            )
49	            for train_seed in range(seeds)
50	        )
51	    return out
52	
53	
54	def _fixed_scores(name: str, values: list[float], seeds: int = 5) -> tuple[ScoreSet, ...]:
55	    """Split a fixed score list evenly over training seeds (pooling restores the list)."""
56	    chunks = np.array_split(np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64), seeds)
57	    return tuple(
58	        ScoreSet(player=name, train_seed=i, scores=chunk, games=())
59	        for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)
60	    )

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r2/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a55f9a4d63db2badd/tests/intent/rllab/test_model_eval.py
"""Model-evaluation tests on real runs (M1-T31): TC-16, TC-26, TC-27 and QR-01 to QR-04."""

import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import load_settings
from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, evaluate
from rllab.lab.build import make_env
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.players import load_player

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
FIXTURES = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "rllab"
SETTINGS = ROOT / "settings"


def _load(case: str) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    folder = FIXTURES / case
    inputs = json.loads((folder / "input.json").read_text())
    expected = json.loads((folder / "expected.json").read_text())
    return inputs, expected


TC16_INPUT, TC16_EXPECTED = _load("TC-16")


@pytest.mark.intent
def test_tc16_corridor_settings_are_the_cases_settings() -> None:
    settings = asdict(load_settings(SETTINGS / "corridor.toml"))
    for key, value in TC16_INPUT["settings"].items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for inner, inner_value in value.items():
                assert settings[key][inner] == inner_value, f"{key}.{inner}"
        elif key != "name":
            assert settings[key] == value, key


@pytest.mark.intent
@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", TC16_EXPECTED["seeds"])
def test_tc16_tabular_agent_solves_the_corridor(
    seed: int, tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)  # run_train writes runs/ under the working directory
    record = run_train(SETTINGS / "corridor.toml", seed)
    assert record.status == "complete"

    agents, settings = load_player(str(record.path), seed)
    n_games = TC16_INPUT["eval_games"]
    spec = EvalSpec(player="corridor", train_seed=seed, n_games=n_games, record=n_games)
    scores = evaluate(make_env(settings), agents, spec)

    steps = np.array([len(game.frames) - 1 for game in scores.games])

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r2/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a55f9a4d63db2badd/tests/unit/rllab/test_model_eval.py
"""Model-evaluation tests on real runs (M1-T31): TC-16, TC-26, TC-27 and QR-01 to QR-04."""

import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import load_settings
from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, evaluate
from rllab.lab.build import make_env
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.players import load_player

pytestmark = pytest.mark.unit

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
FIXTURES = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "rllab"
SETTINGS = ROOT / "settings"


def _load(case: str) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    folder = FIXTURES / case
    inputs = json.loads((folder / "input.json").read_text())
    expected = json.loads((folder / "expected.json").read_text())
    return inputs, expected


TC16_INPUT, TC16_EXPECTED = _load("TC-16")


def test_tc16_corridor_settings_are_the_cases_settings() -> None:
    settings = asdict(load_settings(SETTINGS / "corridor.toml"))
    for key, value in TC16_INPUT["settings"].items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for inner, inner_value in value.items():
                assert settings[key][inner] == inner_value, f"{key}.{inner}"
        elif key != "name":
            assert settings[key] == value, key


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", TC16_EXPECTED["seeds"])
def test_tc16_tabular_agent_solves_the_corridor(
    seed: int, tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)  # run_train writes runs/ under the working directory
    record = run_train(SETTINGS / "corridor.toml", seed)
    assert record.status == "complete"

    agents, settings = load_player(str(record.path), seed)
    n_games = TC16_INPUT["eval_games"]
    spec = EvalSpec(player="corridor", train_seed=seed, n_games=n_games, record=n_games)
    scores = evaluate(make_env(settings), agents, spec)

    steps = np.array([len(game.frames) - 1 for game in scores.games])
    reached = scores.scores >= settings.rewards.food
    in_time = reached & (steps <= TC16_EXPECTED["per_seed"]["max_steps"])
    assert len(scores.scores) == n_games
    assert in_time.mean() >= TC16_EXPECTED["per_seed"]["goal_rate_min"]

  "command": "uv run --frozen python -c \"\nimport numpy as np\nfrom rllab.envs import Snake, COMPACT_FIELDS\nfrom rllab.baselines.scripted import _choose\nenv = Snake(view='compact')\ndef play(policy, seeds):\n    tot=0\n    for s in seeds:\n        obs,_=env.reset(s); over=False\n        while not over:\n            k=obs['agents'][0].tobytes()\n            a=policy.get(k)\n            if a is None: a=_choose(obs['agents'][0]); policy[k]=a\n            r=env.step({'agents':np.array([a])}); obs=r.obs; over=r.terminated or r.truncated\n        tot+=r.info['score']\n    return tot/len(seeds)\npol={}\nseeds=list(range(300))\nbest=play(pol,seeds); print('scripted', best, len(pol), flush=True)\nfor it in range(2):\n  for k in list(pol):\n    orig=pol[k]\n    for a in range(3):\n        if a==orig: continue\n        pol[k]=a; v=play(pol,seeds)\n        if v>best+0.05: best=v; orig=a\n    pol[k]=orig\n  print('pass', it, best, flush=True)\ntest=list(range(5000,5500)); print('test', play(pol,test), 'scripted test', play({},test), flush=True)\nfor k,a in pol.items():\n    o=np.frombuffer(k,dtype=np.float32)\n    if a!=_choose(o): print([f for f,v in zip(COMPACT_FIELDS,o) if v], 'scripted', _choose(o), 'best', a)\n\"",

1	"""The value networks: one output per action from a flattened observation (AD-05)."""
2	
3	import math
4	
5	from torch import nn
6	
7	from rllab.core import AgentParams
8	
9	
10	def build_net(obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], n_actions: int, params: AgentParams) -> nn.Module:
11	    """AD-05's network for `params.net`: the compact-view MLP or the full-grid CNN."""
12	    if params.net == "cnn":
13	        return _cnn(obs_shape, n_actions, params.hidden)
14	    return _mlp(obs_shape, n_actions, params.hidden)
15	
16	
17	def _mlp(obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], n_actions: int, hidden: int) -> nn.Module:
18	    """Two hidden ReLU layers of `hidden` units over the flattened observation."""
19	    return nn.Sequential(
20	        nn.Flatten(),
21	        nn.Linear(math.prod(obs_shape), hidden),
22	        nn.ReLU(),
23	        nn.Linear(hidden, hidden),
24	        nn.ReLU(),
25	        nn.Linear(hidden, n_actions),
26	    )
27	
28	
29	def _cnn(obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], n_actions: int, hidden: int) -> nn.Module:
30	    """Two padded 3x3 ReLU convolutions (16, 32 channels), then one hidden layer of `hidden` units.
31	
32	    `obs_shape` is `(channels, height, width)`; padding keeps the height and width.
33	    """
34	    channels, height, width = obs_shape
35	    return nn.Sequential(
36	        nn.Conv2d(channels, 16, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
37	        nn.ReLU(),
38	        nn.Conv2d(16, 32, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
39	        nn.ReLU(),
40	        nn.Flatten(),
41	        nn.Linear(32 * height * width, hidden),
42	        nn.ReLU(),
43	        nn.Linear(hidden, n_actions),
44	    )
45
